| 2026-10-13 | Host unresolved-command evidence owns immutable routing, not mutation outcome; only the original owner's valid response discharges its dependency | No-Run commands need original provider/release retention without a competing business receipt | Client-current proxies, execution-only dependency holds, mutable provider retargeting, or host success fabrication |
| 2026-10-13 | Prove the current public backend subset through an isolated file-proxy SDK archive while its release metadata is still pending | Independent build evidence must not rely on Runtime internal source, workspace privilege or local replace; a temporary snapshot must not masquerade as a published artifact | Overwriting a cached published module, claiming an old release contains the new SDK, or publishing before the final authorization boundary |
| 2026-10-13 | Run backend, contract, failure and relevant race checks; do not write/run frontend UI, E2E or screenshot checks | Explicit task verification constraint | Running the composite frontend gate without inspecting its test components |
| 2026-10-13 | Merge current local main into the feature branch after each verified slice; do not push, publish, modify shared main history or use credentialed services during ordinary slices | The amended task explicitly requires incoming main synchronization; external/shared changes and credentials still require authorization | Deferring integration until final delivery or treating code-edit authorization as production authorization |
| 2026-10-13 | Dispatch admission is durably recorded before the network call; subsequent retries only read the original receipt | Transport loss or process death cannot prove whether the effect happened; the original binding survives availability/provider changes | Resending on absent receipt or selecting the current provider |
| 2026-10-13 | Store typed success and known refusal in the private receipt transaction; roll back refused mutation with a savepoint | A known refusal must replay the original decision while unknown commit/storage failures retain uncertainty | Bare success-only results or host business-result caching |
| 2026-10-13 | Keep historical command identities without an arbitrary lifetime-count cap or automatic eviction | Existing private receipts did not cap total historical usage; adding 4096 as a permanent product quota would break ordinary use, while eviction permits identity retargeting | Unmeasured permanent history quotas or expiration into a new provider call |
| 2026-10-13 | Use immutable Go-owned method contracts and Scope's existing schema derivation for authoring; reuse Runtime's TS emitter | Actual Reviews server, Go caller, Tool schemas and generated consumers need one structural owner | Parallel hand-maintained DTO/Schema/TS, a second SDK module or a custom IDL compiler |
| 2026-10-13 | Keep descriptor and generated files as projections; validate raw input and output at the bound method | Typed decoding can hide absent fields; artifact edits must not change a running method or create another registration path | Revalidating only a re-encoded DTO or classifying malformed output as a known refusal |
| 2026-10-13 | Give Reviews a read-only projection of its existing private invocation receipts | Recovery must not re-enter the mutation owner; the stored outcome and original arguments already supply the needed evidence | A new receipt/result table, same-key mutation used as a receipt query, or treating missing evidence as permission to resend |
| 2026-10-13 | Carry one admitted raw invocation fingerprint to private receipt owners; preserve numeric lexemes and normalize object order at the common boundary | Struct/map encoding and raw/decoded numeric encoding do not agree today; recomputing from a DTO can change identity | Map-only ordering, float64 canonicalization, backend DTO rehashing, silent same-schema hash changes or a compatibility hash fallback |
| 2026-10-13 | Persist immutable admitted JSON in host routes and private receipts; derive the digest instead of persisting a competing DTO hash | Original input must survive decoding, restart and receipt lookup; the actual input_json format change makes strict cutover enforceable | Changing hash semantics under the old schema, retaining only a re-encoded DTO, introducing a per-record epoch or keeping the former helper as an alias |
| 2026-10-13 | Share the pure public jsoninput value across Domain and SDK authors | Domain cannot depend on the MCP/Scope transport package; independent authors need the same encoding owner without Runtime internal source | Duplicated canonicalization implementations, importing transport into Domain or creating a separate SDK module |
| 2026-10-13 | Publish immutable typed exports through one public backend invocation/discovery transport | Installed operations need schema-bearing exports without connection code importing every feature's Go DTOs | Per-feature client registration, mutable published exports, former named-method aliases or a reflection/ambient service registry |
| 2026-10-13 | Keep Schedules settlement as the existing convergent accepted-Run handoff with one captured backend and stable captured RunID identity; Stage 3d carries it through Dispatch and deletes the old remote Accept entrance | Runtime's durable acceptance and the private pending queue already own recovery; an absent host dispatch record must not prevent settling that proven Run | Treating acknowledgement as another Run creation, current-provider retargeting, or replacing handoff ownership with a host business result |
| 2026-10-13 | Commit Memory maintenance receipt at its actual publication owner, with model I/O outside transactions | The new outer receipt transaction prevented concurrent human editing and rolled back required pending ledger evidence | Holding a database transaction through model calls, weakening the curation race test, or fabricating successful host maintenance evidence |
| 2026-10-13 | Persist private admission before maintenance model I/O; infer pending from absent reply and complete with publication in its existing transaction | Restart or response loss cannot authorize another model request; partial committed ledger evidence must survive while publication and receipt remain atomic | An in-memory running flag, retrying an absent receipt, a second checkpoint/result store, or rolling ledger facts back through model I/O |
| 2026-10-13 | Keep target meaning declared by the contract; capture concrete target/origin/parent at generic host admission next | Current Reviews uses installation-private facts; target metadata alone is not captured invocation authority | Pretending backend descriptors already implement installed provider/context binding |


## Review closure

This is a reconciliation with the original review, not a blanket closure.
Production implementation remains `9f5a66d51`; the diff through `cae633a29`
contains only tests and documentation. Complete normal/original parallel race,
vet, shared TS, standalone author artifacts and Runtime/native qualification
retain the exact source and test scope recorded in Current stage. The later
Core/private CLI identity cases have separately scoped focused proof; they are
not an invented repeat of every gate. `b2dcbfbba` remains the earlier source/API
closure milestone. Packages without tests and guarded live/platform scenarios
are distinguished from executed tests. Graphical proof remains unexecuted, not
satisfied by exclusion, source checks or native compilation. The partial
requirements below prevent whole-proposal completion and public-contract freeze
qualification.

| Review requirement | Current implementation and evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Accurate baseline and owner boundaries | Last integrated main `7ba40c117`, released Scope v0.50.0; Runtime/Scope and private backend facts retain their original authorities. Full package/architecture checks pass. |
| Remove closed service/view and privileged feature paths | Generic declarations, capabilities, bindings and resources replace closed business enums/carriers. Public Runtime build closure contains no private feature implementation. `6b3a1d823`, `9f2bac554`, `2079ee06f`, `6d3728a0c`, `03fb6eeb6` delete the remaining private receipt/read/model/context/client dependencies. |
| Shared Tool/CLI/UI/cross-plugin operations | Actual Reviews, Memory and Schedules consumers enter the same backend operations and private receipts. Source API closure is 95/95; complete backend/CLI and shared-client checks pass. Graphical interaction is excluded. |
| Origin, parent, target and lifetime | Installed operation, Tool preparation, background capture, model callback and active-member tests pass. Memory Search's detached embedding parent was RED/GREEN fixed in `6d3728a0c`; restored attribution never manufactures an execution publisher. |
| Collection/selection/decoration and plugin points | Exact consumer contracts, immutable setup, cycle checks, explicit compositions and actual Reviews/analyzer process lifetimes pass. CLI `33696b3c2` adds real composition inspection/selection/resolution; transitive failures retain their complete consumer path. No second readiness ledger. |
| Reentrant RPC, remote next and streams | Actual SDK/headless processes verify reentrancy, frozen remaining chains, lazy consumption, acknowledgement backpressure, cancellation, byte budgets and stale grant refusal. The first remote model observer is explicitly transparent; its once-through rule is not universal middleware policy. |
| Final transformed Tool arguments | `6a88bd45a` adds the public installed contract and actual stdio/Reviews normal proof across approval and Runtime restart. Final arguments, private mutation and canonical history agree; invalid proposals do not execute. Current global race/artifact requalification is recorded in Current stage. |
| Independent model work and accounting | Each physical exchange has its own identity, captured route, caller budget and durable source. Source/Run/Goal tests preserve known inner usage under outer failure and unknown consumption under restart/cancellation. Independent output cannot alter primary conversation/context. No invented universal Goal ceiling. |
| Model selection/context/input/counter admission | Primary/auxiliary/installed request tests preserve captured order/selection, authored input, final media/input/output capacity and counter failure/cancellation. Original sources remain evidence; providers are never automatically replayed. |
| Ordinary embedding access and Memory-specific limits | Public text embedding and Tool context replace private metadata/methods. Ordinary installed callbacks support multiple embedding exchanges; Memory's operation grant consumes one generated published caller policy. Absence, configured errors and storage failures retain distinct semantics. |
| No-Run unknown commands and original receipts | Installed Reviews now also exercises Runtime response loss, cold store reopen, disable/reenable, pending update/configuration/uninstall holds, replacement-target conflict and exact private receipt recovery without a Run or mutation redispatch. Memory selection tests and shared-client replacement/journal tests remain separate proofs. The actual UI plus Runtime restart/update/uninstall/selection cross-scenario has not run. Host stores routing, never competing business success. |
| One contract generation source and numeric parity | The review's explicit Go-owned alternative was selected in the decision ledger; Schema-first Go input was not implemented or claimed. Typed methods generate canonical Schema, Go projections and TS, with exact current regeneration. Feature JSON and the shared Go/TS corpus cover wide numbers through unary/stream/journal/point paths. This is feature JSON parity, not a universal arbitrary-precision conversion of every Core DTO. `9f5a66d51` completes pure typed admission before fresh route binding for Schema-valid decimal/exponent integer spellings, with real CLI RED/GREEN, unary/stream identity and unchanged receipt recovery proof. |
| Typed refusal versus uncertainty | Real Tool/CLI query/revision-conflict facts agree. Current Reviews removes the incorrect lifetime receipt quota and its unrecorded refusal. Store/SDK and independent model Tool/CLI proofs retain new successful work and original known-refusal recovery beyond 4,096 historical receipts; errors without evidence remain unknown. The real CLI original-binding conflict is `idempotency_conflict`, with exact Go classification and original-receipt preservation after later mutation. The separate actual model/CLI private `invocationConflict` case preserves prior private receipts, explicitly refuses different-input recovery and permits a fresh command without replay. Rendered comparison is unexecuted; the cross-scenario remains Partial. |
| Public components/shared modules/resources | Ordinary component ESM, relative/authenticated exact-release resources, host module maps and shared UI/React/context ownership are implemented and type/native built. The complete packed Usage feature builds outside the checkout with identical resource bytes; graphical identity/interaction is not executed. |
| Management independent of failed optional UI | Partial: independent CLI and built-in metadata management exist; blocked-backend inspection/disable is tested. Recovery while module initialization blocks the actual renderer remains unexecuted. An error boundary or source separation does not complete that review scenario. |
| Protocol and activation identity | Current protocol requirements, complete export sets and immutable same-release activation proofs are tested. SDK additions do not infer a new public wire epoch from internal build versions. Retired shapes are explicitly refused. |
| Update/withdrawal/retention/uninstall | Original pending commands and waiting executions retain exact transitive release dependencies. Candidate failure, explicit selection, disable/re-enable, independent source origin and retained private namespaces are tested. Old directories are preserved, never converted or adopted by another owner. |

Whole bootstrap and all eight crash cuts pass in normal and full Runtime race suites. Core Run acceptance survives unavailable source/observation loss; no host scheduler or provider replay remains. |

| Install integrity/credentials/core protections | Complete package/archive/namespace/secret/source/Tool-authority/file/process tests remain in the backend suites. Deleted guest isolation tests do not replace or remove these sound protections. |
| Independent authors and current distributions | Current `9f5a66d51` independent Reviews/Tool-proposal/command-next author offline tidy/verify/race/vet/build passes with workspace off, no replace and explicit unreleased module archives. Required internal implementation and companion localruntime are included; authored code has no private imports. Current Runtime/catalog/native packaging passes with pinned task-local beta.29 and a fresh Vite build. Current packed Usage also reproduces formal component bytes outside the repository, including offline clean reinstall. Registry publication, full offline release-metadata inventory, all-platform execution and graphical qualification remain absent. |
| Final deletion and documentation | `b2dcbfbba` closes source/API/doc cutover against the original replacement map; P13's public installed boundary, active guidance and generated inventory are present. Architecture/generation/static checks pass. Current global/native qualification and the separately unexecuted graphical criteria are not completed by P48 source closure. |

## Proposal acceptance coverage

Audit date: 2026-10-15; production implementation: `9f5a66d51`; latest focused
CLI proof: `cae633a29`; source/API closure milestone: `b2dcbfbba`. Scenario titles
and expected behaviors are rechecked against the original proposal and review,
rather than substituted with easier checks.
**35 Evidenced / 13 Partial / 0 Open** is a count of evidence coverage, not a
percentage of implementation or a claim of release readiness.

`Evidenced` means matching executable evidence is recorded and the relevant source
or test was checked in this audit. It does not mean a fresh invocation occurred
in this audit. `Partial` means an original behavior is only partly demonstrated;
`Open` identifies known unfinished work. The complete normal and original
parallel-race selections recorded in Current stage each finish all 174 selected
packages with unchanged production implementation `9f5a66d51`: 159 tested
packages pass, fifteen have no tests, and seven existing paid/subprocess tests
skip. The normal and race test sets differ, and later test-only additions have
their own scoped qualification above. No full-selection rerun follows the
private-conflict addition at `cae633a29`. Current-production vet, shared TS,
architecture, Runtime/native, independent Go author and freshly packed Usage
results are recorded in Current stage; `5ff75fffc` results retain their frozen
earlier scope. Platform/conditional test execution is not inferred from package
summaries. The user has since authorized graphical execution. The new actual
Web/Wails evidence above closes only P29/P32; other criteria retain their exact scope.

| Requirement | Original scenario | Status | Evidence and remaining work |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Current independent Reviews plus Tool-proposal/command-next author tidy/race/vet/build also pass with workspace off, no replace and local module archives. ## Unresolved questions

P13 and direct installed composition/inner-mutation/disconnection/CLI-host proofs
(P12/P16/P18/P41) now have matching executable evidence. P48 source/docs/API cleanup
is committed. Thirteen partially demonstrated graphical rows and all four review
cross-scenarios remain open for acceptance. The user prohibition prevents graphical execution; it does
not waive the original requirements. See the individual table for exact limits.

The delegated-approval deadline and waiting-tree authority defect have causal
production repairs and a passing original complete parallel gate. That result
stands independently of the withdrawn overall completion claim. The post-SDK
safe-steer timeout remains a historical failure without a separately established
cause. Stage observations cover normal activation, Tool, commits and dispatch;
four negative controls show that distinct finished failures/losses can produce
the same caller timeout. Current complete CLI/root binding race passes, with
failure-only diagnostics retained. These observations do not establish which
cause occurred historically or timing margin under other contention. No
assertions, original deadlines or event-consumption order were relaxed.

Retention remains conservative: Run-linked evidence follows its Run, Session-only
evidence follows atomic Session deletion, and unattached evidence is retained.
Pending/unknown commands never expire into replay permission. No new TTL or
universal Goal cost/step ceiling was inferred from silence.

Graphical interaction remains unexecuted by user instruction. The earlier
interrupted prohibited UI run is not current acceptance evidence and is not
claimed resolved. Earlier public artifact qualification used branch-source archives
and an offline external Go module. The earlier packed Usage artifact remains
unchanged-source evidence for its recorded snapshot. Current `9f5a66d51` packed
Usage and independent Reviews/Tool-proposal/command-next author qualification
pass after the typed-input admission repair. The public CLI RED/GREEN case closes
that structural boundary; it does not explain either historical deadline.
The current full Runtime module archive includes its internal implementation and
companion localruntime artifact; author source imports only public APIs. An archive
of public directories alone is insufficient for this current SDK and is not
claimed as a new pass.
Registry publication and external deployment are not authorized or claimed.

## Spillover risks

- Complete typed structural input admission has its shared unary/stream repair,
  public CLI RED/GREEN proof and current complete ordinary/parallel/artifact/native
  qualification. The later test-only identity case passes focused race; it is not
  added to the earlier full race's count. Current actual executions are kept
  distinct from frozen preceding-source gates.
- The native build's existing npm prepare wrote shared
  `core.hooksPath=desktop/.husky/_` without a captured prior value. That value is
  preserved rather than guessed away; future task-owned qualification sets
  `HUSKY=0`. The primary checkout's source and user stash are untouched.
- Current Core is `2026-10-15.2`, backend protocol is
  `io.github.tangerg.flame/backend/2026-10-15.3`, and trajectory export schema is 3.
  Rebuild affected Go/TS/component/backend consumers together. Old wire shapes,
  private scope metadata and retired APIs have no reader or compatibility alias.
- Strict host schema/checkpoint admission refuses former activation-only routing,
  missing logical purpose and other replaced shapes before writes. Preserve old
  directories and finish old waiting work with its original binary. No production
  data conversion, copying or deletion was performed. Unchanged private backend
  data remains bound to its original owner; retention is not automatic migration.
- Unknown business/provider outcomes retain original identity, input and source.
  Missing receipt, disabled source, lost observation or a new selection cannot
  authorize replay. These guarantees remain in the complete backend suites.
- Registry/module publication is absent. Independent authors must use the
  qualified current-source artifacts until an authorized release provides them;
  older published application module metadata is not evidence of the new SDK.
- Native package qualification is an arm64 production build and ad-hoc signature
  check with a macOS 13.0 floor. No UI launch, rendered Hooks/provider identity,
  remote graphical workflow or graphical reload lifecycle was executed.

## Deliberately excluded scope

- Frontend UI, E2E and screenshot test execution or additions.
- Marketplace, untrusted-code sandbox, arbitrary workflow DSL, automatic compatibility, live provider migration and a second execution engine, as deferred by the proposal.
- Production operations, user-data deletion, destructive Git actions, credentialed services or publishing without the required authorization.
